Applied Materials, Inc. (AMAT-US)  
財報 FIRST TAKE — AI 產能大基建的最大贏家，財測超標但「不夠驚喜」

個股基本資料： 
Applied Materials, Inc. (AMAT-US) 
日期 2026/8/14 
收盤價 $548.15 
股價表現 
三個月報酬(%) 22.43 
六個月報酬(%) 50.61 
一年報酬率(%) 181.29

資料來源：Factset，Bloomberg 
 評論及分析 (Overall Summary) 
Applied Materials, Inc. (AMAT) 2026 財年第三季 (Q3 FY2026) 財報展現公司
受惠於全球 AI 基礎設施的快速建設與對先進晶片需求激增的強勁動能。憑藉
在 AI 運算相關高效能與高價值技術領域的領導地位，公司實現創紀錄的單季
營收與獲利，並創下歷史最高的季增長率。在半導體系統與全球服務業務  
(AGS) 雙引擎發力推動下，單季營收達到 91 億美元。儘管面臨建廠無塵室空
間受限與短期擴產的人力成本挑戰，但客戶為確保供應鏈穩定，積極提供長
達八個季度的滾動預測，針對技術藍圖的能見度甚至延伸至 2030 年。隨著業
界迅速朝向先進邏輯製程、 DRAM 及先進封裝  (Advanced Packaging) 發
展，Applied Materials 透過其深厚的材料工程技術與 EPIC 創新平台，已進一
步鞏固其市場領導地位。展望 2026 財年第四季，在 DRAM 強勁復甦與領先
晶圓代工邏輯製程推動下，公司預期營收將再次大幅跳升，持續作為 AI 發展
趨勢下的主要受惠者。 
公司財報重點數據： 
Applied Materials 在 2026 財年第三季展現強勁的財務表現，單季總營收達 
91 億美元，不僅季增 15%，年增更達 25%，創下歷史新高。受惠於價值定價 
(Value-based pric ing) 策略及高利潤產品組合， Non-GAAP 毛利率微升至  
50.4%，較上季成長  40 個基點，較去年同期增加  150 個基點；同時  Non-
GAAP 營業利益率也擴張至 34% 的歷史新高，年增 330 個基點。亮眼的營運
表現帶動第三季 Non-GAAP 每股盈餘 (EPS) 達 3.50 美元（+41%YoY）。 
公司業績表現： 
⚫ 半導體系統 (Semiconductor Systems) 業務：營收達創紀錄的 70 億美元 
(+27%YoY)。其中，DRAM 營收 (包含 HBM 封裝) 較去年同期增長 52% 
創下新高。在沉積 (包含 PVD、CVD 及磊晶)、材料改質、材料移除 (蝕刻
與 CMP) 以及製程診斷與控制 (PDC) 等多個領域皆創下營收紀錄。該部門 
Non-GAAP 毛利率升至 55.4%，Non-GAAP 營業利益達 27 億美元。 
⚫ 全球服務 (Applied Global Services, AGS) 業務：營收達 18 億美元 (年增 
22%)。受惠於訂閱服務成長與高交易性零件需求，並利用  AI 與倉儲自動
化提升營運效率，部門毛利率達 35.6%，營業利益率達 30.1%。目前全球
已有超過 37,000 個機台連接至其專屬的 AIx 軟體平台。 
⚫ 其他業務 (Other)：營收約 2.94 億美元，主要為顯示器 (Display) 業務。

複委託報告 August 14, 2026

### Page 2

2
公司展望與產業前景：
⚫ 2026 財政年度第四季指引 ：預估營收將落在  102.5 億美元區間  (+
51%YoY)。半導體系統營收預計約 79 億美元 (+62%YoY)，AGS 營收約
18.4 億美元  (年增  22%)。Non-GAAP 毛利率預估約  50.4%，Non-
GAAP EPS 預計為 4.02 美元 (+85%YoY)。
⚫ 先進封裝與 PDC 前景：預期 2026 日曆年的整體先進封裝營收將成長超
過 70%，而製程診斷與控制 (PDC) 業務在今年也將成長超過 50%。
⚫ 產能擴張與中國市場 ：為了應對強勁的市場需求，公司本季新增超過
1,500 名製造與客服人員，並計畫在 2028 年前將系統產出能力翻倍。中
國市場本季佔半導體系統與 AGS 總營收的 26%，在 28 奈米代工邏輯投
資推動下，預期今年將持續成長。
電話會議 Q&A 重點摘要：
⚫ 訂單能見度與長期合約 ：管理層指出，針對先進晶片供不應求的情況，
大型客戶提供了長達八個季度的詳細滾動式預測，部分關鍵客戶針對技

術藍圖的能見度更延伸至  2030 年。這種高能見度顯著改善了公司與供
應鏈的產能規劃。
⚫ 毛利率與定價策略動態 ：分析師關注毛利率在營收大幅增長下於下一季
度的指引為何持平。管理層解釋，短期內因大量招募工程師與服務人員
產生了「爬坡成本」(Ramp costs) 抵銷了部分規模經濟優勢。然而，透
過「價值定價」(Value-based pricing) 策略，半導體系統毛利率已穩居
55% 以上，長期來看整體毛利率仍將保持增長趨勢。
⚫ ICAPS 與各終端市場復甦：針對 ICAPS ( 物聯網、通訊、汽車、電源與
感測器) 市場，公司預期該領域的設備消化期已接近尾聲，隨著客戶端
產能利用率上升以及電源、光子晶片的亮點需求，預期 ICAPS 整體在今
年及明年皆將重返成長軌道。
公司簡介：
Applied Materials, Inc. 是全球領先的半導體與顯示器設備及材料工程解決方
案供應商。該公司致力於推動資料中心、人工智慧  (AI) 運算基礎架構與先進
應用的底層晶片製造技術。其廣泛的產品線涵蓋沉積、材料移除、先進封裝

至製程診斷控制系統 (PDC)，並透過 Applied Global Services (AGS) 提供高
度依賴  AI 預 測 分 析 的 機 台 最 佳 化 解 決 方 案 。 近 期 更 透 過 設 立 EPIC
(Equipment and Process Innovation and Commercialization) 中心，進一
步深化與頂尖晶片製造商及研究機構的早期共同創新 (Co-innovation)。
競爭對手與公司自身價值：
Applied Materials 在全球半導體製造設備市場中面臨眾多同業的競爭。相較
於競爭對手， AMAT 的核心價值與護城河在於其最廣泛且高度互補的技術組
合 (特別在先進邏輯製程、DRAM 及先進封裝)。該公司透過全新的 EPIC 平台
讓客戶在架構發展的極早期階段便能接觸最新設備創新，從而加速新技術導
入並提升晶圓廠的生產良率與單位面積產出。這種深度的技術整合與協助客
戶提升廠區投資報酬率的能力，使其在 AI 軍備競賽中擁有無可取代的產業地

位。

業外淨收入遠優於預期，獲利達成率 111%，單季稅後
EPS 7.11 元，累計 1H26 稅後 EPS 12.36 元 
廣達公告 2Q26 營收 1.036 兆元 、+28%QoQ、+106%YoY，
Server 及 NB 兩大產品線均強勁季成長雙位數，其中
Server 成長高於 NB，毛利率 5.02%(原 5%)、
+0.24pptsQoQ、-2.03pptsYoY，毛利率在 1Q26 新舊品
交替 ASP 變動顯著影響，2Q26 影響已和緩，Server 機
櫃 ASP 大致持平，但仍受到 Memory、CPU 及其他材
料成本上升影響，營業利益 342 億元、+48%QoQ、
+68%YoY，達成率 102%，略優於預期，其中費用率持
續創新低來到 1.72%、-0.22pptsQoQ，主要受惠營收規
模快速擴大帶來營運槓桿，營益率 3.3%(原 3.25%)、
+0.45pptsQoQ、-0.75pptsYoY， 業外淨收入23.17億元，
高於預期，其中匯兌收益高達 32.73 億元、利息淨支出
16.93 億元 ， 稅前淨利365 億元 、+49%QoQ、+69%YoY，

率 111%， 單季稅後EPS 7.11 元(原 6.37 元)。 累計1H26
營收 1.845 兆元、+86%YoY，營業利益 572 億元、
+27%YoY，稅前淨利 610 億元、+30%YoY，稅後淨利
498 億元、+37%YoY，稅後 EPS 12.36 元。

比重約 75~80%，ASIC 比重仍低僅占 AI Server 約 10%；
2Q26 NB 季增雙位數，淡季不淡  
Server 占營收比重已穩定超過 80%，1H26 AI Server
占 Server 營收比重約 75~80%，目前前 AI Server 主要
營收來自 Top 5 CSP 客戶，隨著逐步擴展 NeoCloud 及
ASIC 客戶 ， 有利於改善客戶集中度並提高產品多元性，
NeoCloud GPU Server 及 CSP ASIC Server 專案均已
小量出貨，惟 ASIC 目前占 AI Server 比重仍<10%。 
2Q26 NB 營收占營收比重約低雙位數，出貨量約 1,150
萬台、+15%QoQ、-5%YoY，顯著優於公司原先預期的
持平或小幅成長，亦優於過去 2Q 季節性增幅，主要係
因品牌客戶預期 Memory 價格持續上漲，產能受到 AI 
Server 排擠，而提前備貨。雖如此，但 Memory、CPU
等零組件缺貨及價格持續上漲 ， 仍壓抑NB 毛利率表現。 
2Q26 智慧駕駛及電動車相關產品營收均呈雙位數季成
長及年成長，顯示車載產品組合調整大致已告一段落，
終端市場亦逐步回溫，因 AI Server 營收快速擴大，車
載業務營收占比被稀釋至低個位數，但仍持續取得新專
案，並規劃擴充德國智慧駕駛相關產線。 
三、因應未來AI 龐大需求，除持續擴增美國加州及泰國
廠外，今年底產能倍增，擬取得友達華亞廠因應 2028
年後的需求，資本支出由 300 億元上修至 400 億元 
2Q26 資本支出 136 億元，累計 1H26 資本支出 218 億
元，主要用以美國加州廠及泰國廠的廠房設備擴充，占
資本支出比重約 8 成 ， 公司持續進行AI 相關產能擴充，
2026 年年底達產能倍增的目標(相對 2025 年底)。 同時，
廣達擬以 197 億元取得友達光電的華亞廠，該廠預計
4Q27 完成點交 ， 用以支應2028 年的 Server 訂單需求，
該廠具備較高的電力容量配置，對高階 AI Server 快速
擴充產能具有關鍵優勢，該廠加入後，有利於 2028 年
台灣 AI Server 後段組裝產能倍增，亦因此廣達調整
2026 年資本支出由 300 億元上修至 400 億元。

展望 2026 年，NB 出貨量維持先前與產業平均相當的看

六、維持買進理由 
投顧預估 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 27.79 元及
35.87 元，目前股價325 元，PER <12X 及 9X 不高，短
期毛利率雖仍承壓、但營益率已回穩，隨 VR 2H26 及
1H27 出貨，毛利率、營益率仍舊承壓，惟營益金額仍能
顯著年成長 ， 故PER 仍難回到過去榮景 ， 隨營收環境的
成長無虞，股價基期偏低下，仍有利走勢，故維持買進
投資評等，目標價維持 420 元(PER 15X 2026EPS)。

個股報告 
廣達(2382) 
Neocloud與 CSP伺服器需求能見度樂觀 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $435 
前次投資建議：無

報告日期：2026.08.14 研 究 員：陳威仲

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 2027~28 年伺服器需求維持正向看法，使筆電衰退影響獲利程度有限，目前股價分別位於
2026/27 年 EPS 的 11.8/8.2X，過去股價區間落在 8~29X，目標價 435 元 (13X 
26~27(F)PER)，給予【買進】投資評等，潛在上漲空間 33.8%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/8/13 收盤價： 325.00 ➢ 26Q2 營收 1.0 兆元(QoQ+28.1%/YoY+105.6%)；毛利率
5.0%(季增 0.2/年減 2.0ppts)，優於預期 0.4ppts；營業淨
利 342.4億元(QoQ+48.7%/YoY+67.8%)， 稅後淨利286.5
億元(QoQ+35.2%/YoY+69.9%)，EPS 7.43 元。 
➢ 26Q2 廣 達 筆 電 出 貨 量1,150 萬台 (QoQ+15%/ 
YoY-5%)，優於兆豐預期11.6%及公司前季展望預期(原估
持平~小幅季增)，主因新機種上市動能延續至 26Q2，與
客戶預期記憶體價格將持續高居不下，提前啟動拉貨下半
年的拉貨，預估進入 26Q3 筆電出貨量將季減 20%與年
減雙位數比率。 
➢ 展望 26H2， 預期AI伺服器營收仍將逐季成長 ， 惟因26Q2
較高基期，Q3 季增幅度將較 Q2 收斂。預期隨著 ASIC、
NV MGX ETL等新平台產品於26Q4~2027年進入量產階
段，伺服器市場需求能見度已看至 2028 年，廣達亦能掌
握一定程度的組裝市場份額。 
➢ 預估廣達 2026/27 年 EPS 27.5/39.8 元，2027~28 年伺
服器需求維持正向看法，使筆電衰退影響獲利程度有限，
目前股價分別位於 2026/27 年 EPS 的 11.8/8.2X，過去股
價區間落在 8~29X，目標價435 元(13X 26~27(F)PER)，
給予【買進】投資評等，潛在上漲空間 33.8%。

營 運 分 析

廣達為全球最大的 Notebook ODM廠 
廣達電腦股份有限公司成立於 1988 年，總部位於台灣桃園，於台灣、中國(上
海、重慶)、美國(加州)、泰國、越南、墨西哥、德國皆有設立研發/製造中心，
主要經營 Notebook(消費與商用筆電)、Cloud Server(通用型伺服器、存儲與
網通設備)、AI Server(NV 與 AMD等系統平台)、車用電子、醫療與機器人等
硬體產品的設計、製造及服務及售後服務。廣達現為全球最大的 Notebook 
ODM 廠(全球市占率 30%)，並於近年積極於 AI 伺服器系統級組裝之份額中
佔有一席之地，AI 伺服器占整體伺服器營收比例已達 75~85%，公司客戶結
構亦逐步由既有大型 CSP 延伸至 Neocloud與 ASIC 客戶，廣達 AI 伺服器客
戶與平台組合正逐步多元化，有助降低單一客戶或單一 GPU 平台世代轉換
造成的營運波動。

- 3 - 
個股報告 
26Q2毛利與淨利率優於預期 
廣達 26Q2 營收 1.0 兆元(QoQ+28.1%/YoY+105.6%)，伺服器與筆電營收皆
呈雙位數季增，其中伺服器成長幅度優於筆電，主要受惠高階 AI 伺服器出
貨量快速增加。26Q2 AI Server 系統機櫃平均出貨價格與前季大致相當，預
估 26Q2廣達 GB300約出貨 6.5~7千櫃(26Q1約 4~4.5千櫃) ，因此本季 AI 
伺服器營收大幅季增主要來自實際出貨量增加。 
毛利率 5.0%(季增 0.2/年減 2.0ppts)，優於預期 0.4ppts，高階 AI 伺服器量
產出貨的稀釋效應於 26Q1 一次拉高後趨於和緩；營業淨利 342.4 億元
(QoQ+48.7%/YoY+67.8%)，因毛利率高於預期而優於預期 9.4%，稅後淨利
286.5 億元(QoQ+35.2%/YoY+69.9%)，優於預期 18.1%，主因業外收益高於
預期與稅率低於預期，EPS 7.43 元，優於預期的 6.28 元。

26Q2 筆電出貨量優於預期，惟 26H2 出貨量將因記憶體成本高漲而衰退  
26Q2 廣達筆電出貨量 1,150 萬台(QoQ+15%/ YoY-5%)，優於兆豐預期
11.6%及公司前季展望預期(原估持平~小幅季增)，主因新機種上市動能延續
至 26Q2，與客戶預期記憶體價格將持續高居不下，提前啟動拉貨下半年的
拉貨。而 Q2 為傳統 Chromebook 旺季，Chromebook 出貨僅占 26Q2 筆電
出貨的不到 10%，顯示低單價筆電產品受記憶體價格影響，成本壓力大進而
使出貨量大降。預估進入 26Q3 筆電出貨量將季減 20%與年減雙位數比率。

26H2 AI 伺服器維持成長，GPU、ASIC與通用型需求同步推進 
展望 26H2，預期 AI 伺服器營收仍將逐季成長，惟因 26Q2 較高基期，Q3
季增幅度將較 Q2收斂， 預估廣達26Q3/Q4 GB300將出貨約 5.8~6.0/4.5~4.7
千櫃 ， 另外預估26Q4將出貨 VR約 0.8~1.0千櫃與些微的 AMD Helios機櫃 。 
近期從供應鏈觀察到 Neocloud客戶對於新舊平台的 GPU伺服器需求皆顯著
增加，也預估考量新平台維護成本等因素， GB300 生命周期將延長。目前
GPU 伺服器仍為 AI 伺服器營收主要來源，但公司也持續拓展 ASIC 與
Neocloud 客戶。26H1 ASIC 專案占 AI Server 營收比例仍低於 10%，預期
隨著 ASIC、NV MGX ETL 等新平台產品於 26Q4~2027 年進入量產階段，
廣達亦能掌握一定程度的組裝市場份額。而通用型伺服器 2026 年出貨量成
長率甚至有機會高於 GPU 伺服器，主要受惠 CPU供應情況改善與一般伺服
器需求回升，整體伺服器市場需求能見度已看至 2028 年。

預估廣達 26Q3 營收 1.06 兆元(QoQ+2.2%/YoY+113.9%)；毛利率 5.1%(季
持平/年減 1.8ppts)；營業淨利 354.4 億元(QoQ+3.5%/YoY+92.9%)；稅後淨
利 273.4 億元(QoQ-4.6%/YoY+66.4%)，EPS 7.08 元。

### Page 1

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

GCE’s 2Q26 core business (OPI) was 6%/2% lower than GSe/BBG 
consensus, mainly due to the less favorable than expected GM (was 
2.2%/1.4ppt below GSe/BBG consensus) resulting from the 
mismatch on component cost hike vs. GCE’s product pricing hike 
and the increasing ramp up cost in its new Thailand plant (the 
cost-pricing mismatch and high ramping up cost will both be 
solved in 3Q26). However, the company’s net income was 3/8% 
higher than GSe/BBG consensus, thanks to the more favorable tax 
rate (24% vs. GSe/BBG consensus at 32%/32%) driven mainly by its 
increasing earnings contribution from its new Thailand plant 
(Thailand plant tax rate is significantly lower than Taiwan and 
mainland China plants).  
For 3Q26, we expect the company’s revenue to grow by 26% QoQ 
(in-line with 2Q revenue growth) with (1) the 12% more capacity 
value in 3Q vs. 2Q, (2) the increasing contribution from high ASP 
ASIC products, and (3) the much better pricing in 3Q vs. 2Q (we 
expect the company’s overall product pricing to go up by 10-15%+ 
in 3Q). In terms of GM/OPM, we expect the company’s GM/OPM 
to increase to 37.9%/30.3% (up 3.2ppt/3.9ppt QoQ) thanks to 
the much better product mix and pricing condition for its PCB 
products.  
Investment view - GCE continues to be the most competitive AI 
ASIC PCB supplier, with easing yield rate risk on ASIC HDI 
We continue to expect GCE will hold the major share for Trainium 3 
(70%+ from 3Q26) with its smooth ramp up in its new Thailand 
capacity, which we believe will start to gain share in the TPU supply 
chain from the end of 2026 and should see significant improvement 
on the market share (<5% in 2026 to 20-30%+ in 2027), leading to a 
fast capacity expansion speed in coming years (we believe capacity 
volume will grow by ~30% 2025-28E CAGR vs. 7% 2021-25 CAGR). 
Based on our calculation, GCE could hold 15-17% of global AI PCB

### Page 3

market share in 2026-28E while its ASIC PCB market share should be consistently higher 
than 25% from 2026 (to 30-32% in 2027-28E), suggesting not only a solid revenue 
growth outlook, but also imply better profitability in the long term (we believe AI grade 
PCB GM is and will continue to be 45-50%+ vs. GCE 2Q GM at only at ~35%).  
Moreover, the company mentioned its progress on ramping up thick-copper HDI (for AI 
server) capacity is smooth now and management believe the difficulty on producing AI 
HDI products should be similar to 800G switches (we believe the yield rate should be at 
least 70-80%), and we believe the positive comment on AI HDI yield rate suggests more 
opportunity for the company to enjoy an even better market share and profitability in 
coming quarters/years.  
We suggest investors to build position on GCE after a significant de-rating on P/E 
multiple, before the yield rate ramp up on its new AI ASIC MLB/RPCB capacity, as we 
have already seen the company’s stock price de-rate by 5-10x (was 20-25x in 2024-25, 
but the company only traded at 10-11x of 2027E EPS based on our estimate) due to (1) 
investors’ concern on GCE’s AI HDI yield rate (which could impact the company’s market 
share and profitability in the LT), and (2) the relatively slow GM expansion speed 
especially in 1H26. We believe investors’ concern on GCE AI HDI yield rate will gradually 
ease in coming quarters after the company ramps up all of its new high-end HDI 
capacity; also, considering the much better pricing condition and product mix in 3Q, we 
believe investors will start to revisit GCE’s business once they realize the 2Q GM miss is 
only a one-time issue driven by the pricing/cost hike timing mismatch. As such, we see 
attractive upside on GCE (87% implied upside vs. today) with limited risk in the short 
term to long term.  
Maintain Buy on GCE, with a new 12-m TP of NT$1,800 (from NT$1,585).

Earning and TP revision

Earnings revision 
We revise up our 2026/27/28E earnings estimate by 9/14/13% to factor in the faster 
than expected ASP hike from high-end AI server, as well as the much faster than 
originally expected capacity expansion plans (revise up 2026/27/28E revenue estimate 
by 3/7/6%), despite our less positive view on the GM outlook (due to the unfavorable FX 
movement).

### Page 4

TP revision 
Maintain Buy with a new TP of NT$1,800 (from NT$1,585), based on the new 20x P/E 
(revise down from 22x, to factor in investors’ concern on GCE’s AI HDI yield rate; 20x P/E 
is in-line with the AI PCB industry average valuation in the past 3 years),  also rollover of 
our valuation base from 4Q26-3Q27E to 2027E, and factoring in our earnings revision.

Investment thesis and Valuation & Risks

GCE is a key cloud (server+switch) PCB supplier globally with 20%+ market share in 
2022. We expect cloud PCB market demand to grow at a 15% 2024-26E CAGR (far 
stronger than the overall RPCB+HDI market demand growth rate at 5% 2024-26E 
CAGR). We believe GCE’s proactive capacity expansion plan (33% in 2026) and high 
annualized ROI (300%+) will continue to drive the company’s revenue/margin level in the 
coming quarters/years. Additionally, we believe GCE can continue to see strong demand 
from the increasing content per server/switch, as we expect both markets to see a 
20-50%+ layer count increase after the generation upgrade (upgrade to 400G/Eagle 
Stream). We are constructive on GCE’s long-term growth opportunity given its solid 
position in the rapidly growing cloud PCB market, and we are Buy rated on the stock. 
GCE is trading higher than its historical P/E average level driven by strong AI server 
demand, but lower than the TW AI server players’ average.

We hosted NYPCB’s 2Q26 post result on August 11th. Key 
takeaways include: 
(1) NYPCB mentioned it is considering multiple different CAPEX 
plans apart from the new Chiayi plant but most of these plans are 
still under evaluation. For the new Chiayi plant (NT$46.8bn 
investment or ~7% of NYPCB’s market cap today - see here), NYPCB 
mentioned it will focus on the next generation advanced packaging 
substrates, whose pricing per unit can be US$1,000+, depending on 
the actual spec of substrates, and the revenue contribution could 
start from 1H29.  
(2) In terms of the pricing of ABF substrates, the company 
mentioned the ASP growth will accelerate in 2H26 vs. 1H26, while 
NYPCB believes the like for like basis pricing will continue to go up in 
coming quarters, due to the industry supply shortage condition 
(NYPCB doesn’t expect this to be solved in 2027-28 and expects the 
pricing for ABF substrate could continue to go higher every 
quarter/year without any pricing ceiling).  
(3) For BT substrates, the company mentioned the GM went up from 
single digits % in 1Q26 to low twenties % in 2Q26, thanks to the 
20-30% QoQ pricing hike per quarter since 2H25, and NYPCB 
continues to believe the BT substrate pricing will go up by 20-30% 
QoQ per quarter in foreseeable future.  
(4) In 3Q26, the company believes the revenue should go up by at 
least high teens % QoQ and targets to reach 20%+ QoQ if possible. 
In terms of the revenue growth, ABF substrates will be the key 
growth driver for 3Q with more high-end switches IC & AI ASIC IC 
substrate demand and the better pricing outlook, as well as the 
increasing contribution from its Kunshan plant (UTR was 50% only in 
2Q26 vs. 3Q should be 70%+ with increasing demand from high-end 
networking customer).

### Page 3

(5) In terms of the material supply update, NYPCB believes the cost from T-glass will go 
even higher in 2H26 vs. 1H26, and the company believes the shortage on T-glass will not 
be solved in the foreseeable future, considering the acceleration on substrate spec 
migration speed which should continue to result in lower industry average yield rate and 
consume more and more T-glass volume in the long term.  
(6) For the new capacity expansion plans, the company is considering whether to get 
some funding from customers (like other ABF suppliers), but it will focus on bank loan 
first, as the flexibility on capacity usage could be limited if getting the funding from 
customers.  
Maintain Buy on NYPCB, as the key spot price ABF substrate supplier in the market 
which should benefit strongly from the S/D shortage and pricing hike in the 
foreseeable future, with an unchanged TP of NT$2,500 (based on 21x 2028E EPS).

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - NYPCB 
NYPCB manufactures and markets printed circuit boards (PCBs) and integrated circuit 
(IC) substrates (including ABF and BT substrates). We are Buy-rated on NYPCB 
considering the company’s high exposure to BT substrates and non-LTA ABF substrate 
(70%+ of total revenue), where we expect GM/OPM to increase significantly in the 
coming quarters (from breakeven in OPM in 1H25 to 27/41% in 2026/27E) due to the 
favorable substrate pricing outlook. Despite the company’s low exposure to high-end 
ABF substrate industry (<20% of total revenue in 1H25), we believe the expanding 
demand from ASIC AI server and high-end switch IC demand will drive the company’s 
high-end ABF revenue exposure to 40%+ by 2027E, suggesting better GM/OPM outlook 
in the long term. In 2H25-2H26, we believe NYPCB will continue to benefit from stronger 
ABF/BT pricing and should be a key beneficiary of the pricing uptrend considering the 
company’s key focus on BT and non-LTA ABF substrate industries. Moreover, in the long 
term (after 2026), we expect the ABF industry to return to supply tightness, and NYPCB’s 
expanding high-end exposure will lead to a long-term earnings growth outlook (122%

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

Nien Made (8464.TW)
2Q26 OPM Miss on Freight Costs; Sales Acceleration and Margin 
Recovery Intact
CITI'S TAKE 
Nien Made’s 2Q26 earnings missed expectation, mainly due to elevated 
freight expenses and a higher effective tax rate, while its record GPM and 
improving July-26 sales momentum remained encouraging. We view soft 
OPM as transitory as freight costs should ease alongside lower oil prices, 
while accelerating sales growth should provide better op leverage in 2H26E. 
We lower our 26-28E EPS by 5-9% mainly reflecting higher freight expenses 
and opex assumptions and slightly trim our TP to NT$450 (18x NTM P/E). 
We remain positive on Nien Made, supported by its record GPM, 
strengthening sales trajectory and intact 2H margin recovery.      
2Q26 record GPM but high freight costs weigh on OPM — NM reported 2Q26 sales 
of NT$7.9bn, up 3% y/y but 4% below our forecast. EU was the key growth driver, 
with sales rising >16% y/y led by strong momentum in Belgium and Denmark, while 
the US remained soft amid macro uncertainty. GPM reached a record 60.0% up 70 
bps y/y, supported by mix upgrade, and pricing increases on custom products. 
However, OPM declined 380bps y/y to 27.2% owing to higher freight expenses (we 
estimate c.200bps negative impact). Net profit rose 21% y/y to NT$1.4bn, missing 
our/BBG forecast by 29%/23%, also reflecting a higher effective tax rate. 
2H26E sales growth set to accelerate — July-26 sales of NT$3.0bn increased 16% 
y/y, supported by strong growth in both US and EU, pointing to a visible acceleration 
entering 2H26E. Mgmt expects US sales to grow at a MSD y/y for FY26. Given that 
the US sales declined y/y in 1H26, this guidance implies a meaningful rebound 
during 2H26. Europe should remain another growth engine, supported by continued 
strength in Belgium and Denmark. For international markets outside NA, mgmt. 
expects HSD y/y growth with Japan and Australia also offering incremental 
momentum. 
Growth incentive program to reinforce retail partnership — Starting in July-26, 
NM introduced a growth incentive program under which part of the tariff burden will 
be refunded to their retail partners, helping strengthen long-term customer 
relationship and reinforce NM’s channel position. Separately, the company expects 
to receive tariff rebound of c.NT$300mn from the US government, which should 
help boost EPS in 2H26E. (continued)

Implications — We trim our 2026-28E earnings estimates by 5-9%, mainly 
reflecting higher freight costs and opex assumptions. We slightly cut our TP to 
NT$450 from NT$460, based on 18x 2H26-1H27E EPS, which we roll forward from 
2026E. Despite the earnings cuts, we remain positive on Nien Made and view 2Q 
OPM miss as a transitory setback. With oil prices retreating, freight expenses likely 
normalizing and sales growth accelerating, we expect op leverage to improve and 
margin recovery to resume in 2H26E.

Nien Made (8464.TW)
12 August 2026 Citi Research

Nien Made
Company description
Headquartered in Taichung, Taiwan, Nien Made is a leading global window-
covering provider, selling various blinds, shutters, shades and curtains in the
US, Europe and other countries. Nien Made provides both custom-made
window products (71% of sales in 2025) under its own brand “Norman” and
“VENETA” and ready-made products (21% of sales in 2025), selling to big-box
retailers.

Investment strategy
We rate Nien Made shares as Buy. Our investment thesis is based on: 1) the
company's ongoing share gains in custom made market in the US thanks to a
more favorable competition landscape with major peer facing financial issue.
2) The price hike of custom made products this year is able to mitigate the
tariff impact and potentially drive further margin/earnings upsides. 3)
Valuation looks attractive to us as the share is trading at a discount to its

historical average while core business remains solid.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$450 is based on 18x 2H26-1H27E EPS, on par with its
historical average since 2016.
Risks
Downside risks that could impede the Nien Made stock from reaching our
target price include: 1) A higher than expected Fed fund rate resulting in higher
mortgage rates; 2) a weaker than expected US housing market due to a
deteriorating macro environment; 3) a strong rise in raw-material prices; 4) a

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

### Page 1

Summary: Merida posted its 2Q26 results on 12 August after market hours. Its operating 
profit of TWD765m (+175.7% QoQ, +8.4% YoY) beat our and the Bloomberg consensus 
estimates by 60.1% and 44.7%, respectively. However, its 2Q26 EPS was TWD1.06, lower 
than our forecast (TWD1.50) and the consensus estimate (TWD1.69); we attribute this to a 
net loss from its equity investments. Merida plans to hold an investor call on 14 August and 
we will provide an update then.

We have a Buy (1) rating. For more information on this company, please refer to our latest 
memo, 2Q26 revenue beat our and the consensus forecasts, on 10 July 2026.

What’s the impact 
• 2Q26 results review: core business beat but missed on equity investments. 
Merida’s 2Q26 revenue of TWD6,939m (+30.4% QoQ, -0.9% YoY) beat our and the 
Bloomberg consensus estimates by 10.3% and 2.6%, respectively. Its gross margin 
came in at 19.8% (vs. 15.4% in 1Q26; 18.8% in 2Q25), higher than our (16.5%) and the 
consensus (16.4%) estimates, which we attributed to the momentum brought by new 
models and the recovery in Europe with less price discounting and China. Thus, its 
operating margin came in at 11.0% (vs. 5.2% in 1Q26; 10.1% in 2Q25), beating our 
estimate of 7.6% and the consensus estimate of 7.8%. Its operating profit of TWD765m 
(+175.7% QoQ, +8.4% YoY) also beat our and the consensus estimates by 60.1% and 
44.7%, respectively. However, considering the equity investment loss of TWD200m+, its 
net profit of TWD318m (-2.3% QoQ, -19.2% YoY) missed our and the consensus 
estimates by 29.3% and 36.1%. Its 2Q26 EPS was TWD1.06, lower than our forecast 
(TWD1.50) and the consensus estimate (TWD1.69).

• 2026 outlook: revenue to decline YoY while profitability improves — the worst is 
over. By region, Merida sees the demand recovery in Europe and China but some 
uncertainty in the US market. In 2H26, Merida expects the demand recovery to be 
continuous with better product mix to support its profitability.

What we recommend 
We have a Buy (1) rating. The stock is currently trading at PERs of 19.8x/16.1x, based on 
our 2026/27E EPS, vs. its past-5-year one-year-forward PER trading range of 7-33x.

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
MCU: Chip inflation continuing
in 2H26
Key update:
• Flat spot pricing in August:  STMicro's 32-bit MCU and GigaDevice's (GD) 32-
bit MCU spot prices were flat MoM at Rmb16.5 and to Rmb8.5, respectively.
STMicro spot pricing saw a 29% MoM increase, as the company notified
customers of a new round of contract price adjustments effective June 28,
2026. Separately, local MCU companies such as Nationstech continued to

increase prices by another 10-20%, its second round of price hikes this year.
• Cost side pressure continuing in 2H26: As mature node foundry suppliers
allocate more capacity to AI power-related applications, we expect supply to
remain tight for MCU design houses. Recently, some mature node foundries
hiked pricing again; we think MCU companies need to raise prices to pass
through cost increases.
• Strong data center demand, but weak consumer: Data center related

demand such as optical transceiver, battery management and power control
remains strong, while consumer demand has been weak in the MCU market.
As for industrial, demand is good but largely the same as seen last month.
• Stock implication: We prefer large MCU players such as GigaDevice and
WiFi MCU vendors such as Espressif for their long-term edge AI
opportunities. We remain UW on Nuvoton and await GM recovery before
turning more constructive.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Nuvoton Technology Corporation (4919.TW)
Base case, residual income model. We assume a 9.2% cost of equity (beta 1.2, risk-free rate
2.0% and risk premium 6.0%), a payout ratio of 55%, a medium-term growth rate of 7.0%, and
a terminal growth rate of 3.5%.
Risks to Upside
n MCU up-cycle is more sustained than expected.
n China's MCU localization is faster than expected.
n Traction from auto and BMC business is greater.

n Margin improvement is more significant.
Risks to Downside
n MCU up-cycle ends earlier with severe pricing erosion.
n China's MCU localization is slower than expected.
n Traction from auto and BMC business is less.
n Margins contract amid more intensified competition.
GigaDevice Semiconductor Beijing Inc (603986.SS)
Our price target is our base case value, derived from our residual income model. Key assump-
tions: cost of equity of 8.9% (beta 1.1, risk-free rate 2% and risk premium 6%), a payout ratio

of 40%, medium-term growth rate of 16.4%, and a terminal growth rate of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n NOR up-cycle driven by stronger demand
n Superior chip design, leading to increasing exposure to low-density NOR
n Faster-than-expected DRAM development
Risks to Downside
n NOR down-cycle driven by weaker demand
n Inferior chip design, leading to increasing exposure to mid-/high-density NOR
n Slower-than-expected DRAM development
Espressif Systems (688018.SS)

Base case, residual income model. We assume an 8.9% cost of equity (beta 1.1, risk-free rate
2.0% and risk premium 6.0%), a payout ratio of 40%, a medium-term growth rate of 18.5%,
and a terminal growth rate of 4.5%.
Risks to Upside
n China's MCU localization goes faster than expected.
n Gains traction from new customers.
n Margin expansion is more significant.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 1

ab 12 August 2026
Global Research
Micro-Star International
Navigating component headwinds better than
expected
Q226 margin surprisingly held up amid  component cost pressure
MSI reported Q2 sales NT$49.9bn, -11.3% QoQ/-18.6% YoY and below consensus
expectation of 5% sequential decline, due to softer demand as consumers awaits for
better entry point on intensive price hikes. This was in line with our view on slower pull-
ins from higher component costs. That being said, margins held up better than

expected. Gross margin improved 1ppt QoQ to 16.0%, ahead of UBSe/Street's forecasts
of 12.2%/12.8%; while OpM declined slightly from 6.9% in Q1 to 6.6% in Q2. We have
underestimated its  flexibility on material procurement, inventory management, and
pricing adjustments, which had led to sustainable margins. Q2 EPS was NT$3.55,
42%/49% higher than UBSe/Street thanks to better margins. July sales were reported at
NT$19.3bn, +8.9% MoM & flat YoY, suggesting some shipment recovery.

Favourable pricing uplifts from graphic card price hikes and better product mix
Our studies suggest more hefty graphic card price hikes  are likely to hit the market in
H226 as major chipset suppliers seek to pass on higher component cost increases. Mid
to high-end graphic cards, such as Nvidia RTX 50-series retail pricing are looking to surge
by 20-40% as strong AI server demand are taking up most GDDR memory supplies,
leading to intensive price increases for consumer graphic cards. While we anticipate

memory price hikes to somewhat weighing on consumer demand, MB/VGA suppliers
have been showing optimism on MB/VGA shipments into H226E as AI applications drive
device/equipment upgrades with potential demand uptick for holiday seasons. The
industry is taking on rising risks of component price hikes, notably for memory and SSD,
where MB/VGA has been more proactively adjusting selling prices to secure reasonable
profitability. Meanwhile, Micro-Star has noted its first batch of Nvidia N1X chipset

shipment   is seeing strong demand with increasing orders from US/China customers.
MSI's RTX Spark PC is scheduled to launch by end of Q3 and early Q426; and AI server
products are also going through customer qualifications. We project sales to grow 10%
and 4% in Q326E and Q426E, respectively, mainly driven by better graphic card pricing
and better product mix for higher value added products including AI PCs and AI servers.
Valuation: Refresh PT to NT$155 and maintain Neutral

We  lift 2027/2028E sales growth from 7%/9% YoY to 11%/10% YoY mainly to reflect
higher pricing (despite slower volumes) as AI demand will likely continue to drive GPU
price hikes, especially for high-end graphic cards. We raise 2026/2027/2028E EPS by
15%/17%/17%, and lift PT from NT$108 to NT$155, based on 13x 2028E P/E (rolled
over from 12x 2027 P/E), in line with global hardware peers and implies 0.8x PEG.  While
we are seeing improving product mix, MSI stock has gone up 58% since end of Apr,

outperforming its Taiwanese MB/VGA peers Asustek/Gigabyte +44%/+28%.  We see
balanced risk-reward and fairly priced with limited AI exposure, especially for servers.

### Page 2

Micro-Star International UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q:   Will graphics cards sales resume growth?
Likely. Previous UBS Evidence Lab studies showed pricing for NVDA RTX 50 series plateaued in 2025
after its launch, yet we recently has started to see retail market data suggesting pricing is picking up
again for consumer graphic cards.  Mid to high-end graphic cards are looking to surge by 20-40% in

H226 and potentially up further into 2027/2028E as strong AI server demand are taking up most
GDDR memory supplies, leading to intensive price hikes for consumer graphic cards. While we
anticipate memory price hikes to somewhat weighing on consumer demand/volumes, we are seeing
a wider price hikes in major markets including US and China, which could lead to graphic card market
value growth in 2026E.
Q: Will MSI benefit from the rise of AI servers?

Unlikely at the moment. Although we think Taiwan is the foundation of the computing industry and
Taiwan's server system makers command roughly 90% global market share  of server MBs, including
AI servers, MSI has limited AI server exposure as a late comer. Notably, most of MSI's business is driven
by components, which we believe are still highly cyclical and impacted by consumer demand and the
macro environment. We see the company making efforts to win server projects but its server revenue

contribution remains small at below low single digits and will take time to meaningfully contribute.
We still anticipate relatively slow MB/VGA volumes as component price hikes continue to weigh on
consumer demand. UBS forecasts 2026 PC shipment at +1% YoY, holding up on the back of ASPs
+13.5% YoY as OEMs pass on higher memory and CPU costs; and project PC units +2% YoY in
2027E (LINK). We anticipate AI demand will likely continue to drive GPU price hikes, especially for

high-end graphic cards; where better graphic card pricing and better product mix toward higher
value added products including AI PCs and AI servers will be the major revenue driver while margin
sustainability remains to be proven.
Retail website (link) has shown Nvidia's RTX 50 series of Blackwell graphic cards has seen significant
price increase over the past few months, up c.40% already in August, while GeForce RTX
5060/5070/5080 are also seeing modest price increase, mainly driven by memory pricing

adjustments. Asustek and Gigabyte have yet reported Q2 results, but MSI inventories have gone up
by 25%QoQ/30% YoY with DOI up from 90 days in Q126 to 115 days in Q226, suggesting pull-ins
ahead of component price hikes and preparation for  new product launches.
The stock is trading at 14x/13x 2027/2028E P/E, vs. its historical range of 6-16x. We believe its recent
performance (MSI stock  up 58% since end of Apr, outperforming its Taiwanese MB/VGA peers

Asustek/Gigabyte +44%/+28%) has factored in the expectations on stronger consumer graphic card
pricing, but we see balanced risk-reward considering  limited AI exposure, especially for servers.

Established in 1986, MSI leads in graphics card and MB manufacturing, as well as gaming PCs. In
2024, components (including graphics, MBs and monitors)  accounted for 48% of MSI's total
revenue, followed by system products (including notebooks and desktops), at 45%, and other
products (such as enterprise platform solutions, automotive and commercial solutions), at 7%.

NT$215.00 upside +18% +14% +16% 4.1% NT$14
NT$155.00 base +10% +10% +10% 3.8% NT$12
NT$90.00 downside +2% +5% +4% 3.3% NT$9

Source: UBS estimates
Risk to the current share price is balanced (1:1)
Micro-Star International is trading at NT$154.00 (as of 11 Aug 2026)
UPSIDE (NT$215.00):  In our upside scenario, we factor in 1) AI upcycle driving
component shortages leading to price hikes for PC and consumer graphic cards; 2)
stronger-than-expected PC and gaming demand; 3) development into new server
business momentum exceeding expectations; and 4) stronger margins from a favourable

product mix and better operating leverage. We assume 2028E total revenue of NT
$293.0bn, with components and systems to grow at 18% and 14% YoY in 2028E,
respectively, and we assume net margin of 4.1% to arrive at 2028E EPS of $14.10. We
then apply ~15x multiple to 2028 EPS to arrive at $215 upside valuation.
BASE (NT$155.00): We assume 2028E total revenue of NT$266.3bn with components
and systems to grow at 10% and 10% YoY in 2028E, respectively, and we assume net

margin of 3.8% to arrive at 2028E EPS of $12.03. We then apply ~13x multiple to 2028E
EPS to arrive at $155 price target.
DOWNSIDE (NT$90.00): In our downside scenario, we factor in worse-than-expected
PC and gaming demand, a lack of new server business development and weaker margins
from less operating leverage and intensified competition. We assume 2028E total
revenue of NT$$233.4bn with components and systems to grow at 2% and 5% YoY in
2028E, respectively, and we assume net margin of 3.3% to arrive at 2028E EPS of $9.16.

We then apply ~10x multiple to 2028E EPS to arrive at $90 downside valuation.

Company Description
Established in 1986, Micro-Star International (MSI) leads in graphics card and motherboard
manufacturing, as well as gaming PCs. In 2024, components (including graphics,
motherboards and monitors) accounted for 48% of MSI's total revenue, followed by system
products (including notebooks and desktops), at 45%, and other products (such as enterprise

platform solutions, automotive and commercial solutions), at 7%.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target for Micro-Star International (MSI) is based on P/E ratio.
Company-specific downside risks include: 1) weak demand for graphic processors (GPUs) and
gaming PC leading to softer graphic card sales; 2) a weak PC market leading to a slow
motherboard replacement cycle; and 3) intensified margin pressure on pricing competition or
an inventory correction.

Upside risks include: 1) stronger-than-expected demand for GPUs and gaming PCs leading to
substantial growth in graphic card sales; 2) a strong PC market leading to an accelerated
motherboard replacement cycle; and 3) clearer evidence of a structurally sustainable
AI server business supporting a valuation re-rating.
Sector risks: Investing in the technology sector involves a high degree of risk. Rapid
technological changes, increasing competition and exposure to macroeconomic cycles are

among the many risks faced by investors in technology stocks. Moreover, it is extremely
difficult to project the financial results of tech companies, since their operating models are
highly volatile and unpredictable. Finally, valuing technology stocks can prove challenging, as
neither traditional nor non-traditional valuation measures provide much insight into how
these stocks trade.

the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

2Q26 beat on better opex control 
MPI reported its 2Q26 preliminary results (see Exhibit 1) on 12 Aug. 
2Q26 GM of 58.4% (59.4% 1Q26) came in slightly higher vs. GSe of 
57.9%, but inline with cons. of 58.5%. Meanwhile, 2Q26 OpM of 
34.4% exceeded both GSe/cons. of 32.1%/32.9%, driven by better 
cost control and stronger operating leverage. Overall, 2Q26 EPS of 
NT$15.55 came in above GSe and cons. by 7-10%. To note, the 
company will host an in-person analyst meeting on 14 Aug at 2pm 
Taiwan time. 
Set for GM improvement into 3Q26 and 4Q26 
Looking into 2H26, management now guides for 3Q26 revenue 
growth of 0-5% QoQ, followed by a 5-10% QoQ increase in 4Q26. 
We believe the sequential growth into 4Q26 should still be largely 
driven by the continued AI/HPC demand across both VPC and 
MEMS. To note, we expect MPI’s new US CPU customer could start 
to ramp in 4Q26. On the margin front, management expects GM to 
recover toward 1Q26 levels in 3Q26, driven by a more favorable 
product mix and price hikes (which happened in 2Q26, with positive 
effect contributing starting 3Q26), and 4Q26 GM is also now guided 
to be flat QoQ, but does not exclude the possibility for potential 
upside from new customer ramp and higher utilization rates. 
Meanwhile, management maintained its view that the 2026 opex 
ratio should remain within 25-27%, while expecting further 
operating leverage in 2027 despite continued investments in R&D 
and labor with capacity expansion. Overall, we now model 
3Q26E/4Q26E revenue growth of 10.7%/14.3% QoQ, with GM of 
59.8%/60.4%. 
Multiple growth drivers into 2027 
We expect MPI to deliver a strong revenue growth outlook with

continuous margin expansion into 2027E driven by 1) strong growth outlook from AI 
ASIC/networking customers with continuous TAM expansion, 2) significant market 
share gain within new US CPU customer, with the new customer potentially 
becoming the largest single customer in 2027, 3) the ramp of in-house PCB 
production in 3Q27, which could help lower its outsourcing ratio to 50% and help 
improve its profitability, 4) potential further price hike, and 5) emerging CPO 
opportunities. We now expect its revenue to grow by 105% YoY (vs. 61% YoY in 
2026E) with GM improving to 62.2% (vs. 59.6% in 2026E) in 2027E. 
CPO progressing well but qualification takes time 
In terms of the progress on CPO, MPI’s equipment for Insertion 3 are expected to begin 
engineering-sample shipments by around 10 units in 4Q26 to multiple customers 
spanning OSAT, foundry, and fabless clients. As for Insertion 2, MPI has completed 
on-site qualification and is now shipping to clients’ overseas production sites to do 
qualification. Overall, we remain optimistic that CPO will become the standard for 
next-generation AI networking, and we now model MPI’s CPO-related business to 
potentially contribute mid single digit % and high single digit % of its total revenue 
in 2027E/2028E (vs. high single digit % and low teens % previously).

### Page 4

Earnings changes, valuation and risks 
Forecast changes 
We revise down our 2026E EPS by -1.5% as to factor in management’s updated revenue 
growth guidance for 2H26, which came in lower vs. our prior forecasts. For 
2027E-2028E, we revise up EPS by 4.1%/6.2% mainly as to reflect our view of the 
stronger new US customer contribution, and better operating leverage as revenue 
further scales up.

Maintain Buy with TP raised to NT$8,500 from NT$8,000 
Following our upward earnings revisions, we raise our 12-month TP to NT$8,500 (from 
NT$8,000 previously). Our 12-month TP is based on a 40x 2028E discounted P/E 
(unchanged). We apply this to our 2028E EPS, and discount it back to 2027E at a 12.4% 
CoE to arrive at our TP of NT$8,000. Our key assumptions for CoE include: (1) 1.3x beta 
(sourcing Bloomberg), (2) a 4.25% risk-free rate (inline with GS house-view), and (3) a 
market risk premium of 6.25% (in line with our GS house view). This is also inline with our 
WinWay valuation methodology. Overall, our new TP implies c.29% upside from the 
current share price, and we maintain our Buy rating on MPI.

### Page 7

Investment Thesis, Price Target Risks and Methodology

Investment Thesis – MPI (6223.TWO) 
MPI is a leading Taiwan-based probe card provider specializing in CPC (Cantilever Probe 
Card), VPC (Vertical Probe Card), and MEMS (Micro-Electro-Mechanical Systems) Probe 
Card, which are used in the chip probe (CP) stage. The company is the 4th largest probe 
card supplier with c.7% market share (2024), and 57% of its total revenue comes from 
the probe card segment. 
We like MPI as we expect its revenue/earnings CAGR to further accelerate to 69%/95% 
in 2025-28E, driven by 1) continued share gain in vertical probe card (VPC) market via AI 
ASIC, and 2) potential share gain in MEMS probe card market through penetration into 
new both AI and non-AI customers, along with AI ASIC upgrade. MPI is also aiming to 
increase its PCB self-sufficiency to enable shorter product delivery lead time, which 
would provide it with more advantages over peers. The company is now trading above 
its average AI cycle forward P/E; however, with increasing ASP thanks to rising chip 
complexity and shorter product cadence driving frequent probe card upgrade demand, 
we continue to see upside potential to its revenue and earnings growth. We have a Buy 
rating on the name.  
Price Target Risks and Methodology – MPI (6223.TWO) 
Valuation: We are Buy rated on MPI. Our 12-month TP of NT$8,500 is based on a target 
P/E of 40x (1.7x of 3-year average forward P/E) applied to our 2028E EPS, and 
discounted back to 2027E at 12.4% CoE. Our key assumptions for CoE include: (1) 1.3x 
beta (sourcing Bloomberg), (2) a 4.25% risk-free rate, and (3) a market risk premium at 
6.25% (in line with GS house view). 
Key risks to our views: 1) softer AI/HPC demand, 2) slower penetration into new TAM, 
and 3) intensifying competition.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Industry View In-Line
We keep our 3Q26 iPhone build estimate unchanged at 54M units (+4% q/q, -2%
y/y). The slower y/y run rate reflects new model SKUs shifting to the premium
segment (18 Pro/Pro Max and iPhone Fold), while the iPhone 18 model is scheduled

for introduction in 1H27. We continue to expect 7-8M iPhone Fold builds in 2H26 (no
change) and will monitor the ramp-up progress to see if there is any upside or
downside to builds.
We lower our 3Q26 iPad build forecast by 1M units to 12M units (-8% q/q, -14%
y/y), as we see slower builds heading into 2H, likely due to some pull-forward
demand in 1H and impact from rising prices.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Overall July ODM notebook shipments came in 4% below our estimate:
Component constraints remain a bottleneck, but there is evidence of a fade in the 1H
pull-forward demand. However, we do expect product mix to continue to shift
toward higher-end, more-profitable models. On their recent 2Q earnings calls, the

majority of ODMs noted  that 2H NB builds will be weaker than 1H, which is atypical
of historical seasonality (historically NB builds are 2H>1H). We forecast August NB
builds at 9.0mn (+10% q/q, -20% y/y).
We lower our 3Q notebook build estimate by 6% to 27.3mn units (-10% q/q, -
20% y/y), after factoring in latest guidance from the ODMs post 2Q earnings call.
This 10% sequential decline is sub-seasonal vs. the 15-year average seasonality of
+5% q/q in 3Q. Actual shipments will remain reliant on component availability, but

we continue to hear about constraints and expect this to continue to cap low-end
notebook model builds. Quanta commented on its recent earnings call that 2Q is
typically the peak season for Chromebooks, but due to Chromebook's target market
being more price sensitive, as well as component constraints, Chromebook demand
this year in 2Q was sub-seasonal, accounting for <10% of total notebooks builds
during the quarter.
Exhibit 1: Monthly NB shipments  for top five NB ODMs

Source: Company data, Morgan Stanley Research.  E = Morgan Stanley Research estimates.  "Others" are other leaders.

and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Top five ODMs' July 2026 NB shipments were 8.2mn units (-31% m/m, -24% y/y),
4% below our estimate.
We estimate August 2026 NB builds at 9.0mn units (+10% m/m, -20% y/y).
We lower our 3Q26 NB build estimate by 6% to 27.3mn units (-10% q/q, -20% y/
y).

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Update
Quanta Computer Inc.  | Asia Pacific
2Q26 OpM better than feared;
AI should continue to grow
strongly into CY28
2Q26:
• Servers made up 80%+ of total revenue (within this, AI servers are 75-80%;
ASIC is <10% of AI server mix). NB revenue is a low double-digit portion of
the mix (Chromebooks were <10% of NB mix).
Other takeaways
• CY26 AI servers to grow 100%+ y/y (unchanged); AI servers to reach 80% of
total server revenue (unchanged)
• CY26 general servers to grow y/y (unchanged).

• AI server consignment to start picking up in the mix in 3Q26, but will remain
relatively small.
• CY26 OpM to be similar to C1H26 levels (~3.0%).
• New generation VR200 NVL72 racks will be lower-margin vs. GB300, but
will drive higher profit dollars.
• 3Q NB units down close to 20% q/q.
• CY26 NB units down double-digit y/y.
• CY27 AI testing capacity to double, CY28 AI testing capacity to double again
(implying AI server visibility extending into CY28).

Key Takeaways
2Q OpM was 3.3% (+50bps q/q), 10bps above our estimate/20bps above the
Street – and also better than buy-side expectation of approximately +10-20bps

q/q.
While NB demand is weakening in 2H, Quanta continues to sound bullish on AI
servers, expecting capacity to ramp in CY27-28, implying strong order visibility.
Auto demand stabilized, with 2Q revenue growing in double digits q/q and y/y.
Quanta continues to win new projects.
Stay OW - valuation looks attractive at ~11x CY27e P/E vs. its three-year historical
range of 6-18x.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Quanta Computer Inc. (2382.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.0% (beta of
1.2, equity premium of 6.0% and risk-free rate of 1.5%), an 8.5% medium-term growth rate, and
a 3% terminal growth rate.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected NB demand
n Stronger-than-expected Apple Watch demand
n Stronger-than-expected server demand
n Faster-than-expected AI server penetration

Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected NB demand
n Softer-than-expected Apple Watch demand
n Weak margin performance owing to rising labor costs and sales shortfalls
n Fierce price competition in the mega data center segment
n Slower-than-expected AI server penetration

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Document 122: 260814_daiwa_Gigabyte

Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-14
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: Gigabyte released its 2Q26 results after market hours on 14 August. Gigabyte’s 
2Q26 earnings came in at TWD6.6bn (EPS: TWD9.9), which was 42% and 19% higher 
than our and the street’s expectations, respectively. 2Q26 sales came in at TWD144.2bn 
(up 37% QoQ and 41% YoY), 16% higher than our estimate on better-than-expected 
enterprise AI server demand. However, gross margin decreased 2.4pp QoQ to 9.6%, 0.6pp 
lower than the street’s expectation, mainly due to a higher-than-expected contribution from 
AI server revenue. Operating margin similarly dropped 1.4pp QoQ to 5.6%, following the 
gross margin trend but partially offset by the elevated operating leverage on its expanded 
top line. Net profit was boosted by a higher-than-expected FX gain of TWD885m. Looking 
into 3Q26, we expect the revenue contribution from Neo Cloud clients to become more 
significant, and therefore we believe the top line will continue to grow through 3Q26. 
Moving into 2027, we anticipate Neo Cloud demand will strengthen further, driven by 
increased procurement of Nvidia AI servers from these clients. Lastly, the company 
elevated its 2026 capex outlook from TWD2.3-2.5bn to TWD2.8-3.0bn. To support this, 
Gigabyte has announced the issuance of 40-50m new shares, implying a dilution of c.7-
7.5%. We are currently reviewing our model and will provide an update in due course. We 
have a Buy (1) rating on Gigabyte with a 12M TP of TWD422, based on a target PER of 
15x applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key risk: earnings impact from Nvidia’s 
business model change.

Gigabyte’ 2Q26 results highlights

What’s the impact: 
Below is our first take from Gigabyte’s 2Q26 results. We are currently reviewing our model 
and will provide an update in due course.

• 2Q26 results beat on sales and FX gain. Gigabyte’s 2Q26 earnings came in at 
TWD6.6bn (EPS: TWD9.9), which was 42% and 19% higher than our and the street’s 
expectations, respectively. 2Q26 sales came in at TWD144.2bn (up 37% QoQ and 41% 
YoY), 16% higher than our estimate on better-than-expected enterprise AI server 
demand. However, gross margin decreased 2.4pp QoQ to 9.6%, 0.6pp lower than the 
street’s expectation, mainly due to a higher-than-expected contribution from AI server 
revenue. Operating margin similarly dropped 1.4pp QoQ to 5.6%, following the gross 
margin trend but partially offset by the elevated operating leverage on its expanded top 
line. Net profit was boosted by a higher-than-expected FX gain of TWD885m.

Gigabyte: monthly revenue trend and YoY growth

Source: Company

• Business outlook. Gigabyte reported July sales at TWD44.2bn (up 3% MoM and 60% 
YoY), which achieved 34% of our 3Q26 sales forecast (TWD131.2bn; down 9% QoQ but 
up 65% YoY) and 35% of the Bloomberg consensus of TWD127.7bn. With July revenue 
accounting for 23-35% of Gigabyte’s 3Q revenue over the past five years, the run-rate is 
tracking ahead of our expectation. Looking into 3Q26, we expect the revenue 
contribution from Neo Cloud clients to become more significant, and therefore we 
believe the top line will continue to grow through 3Q26. Moving into 2027, we anticipate 
Neo Cloud demand will strengthen further, driven by increased procurement of Nvidia AI 
servers from these clients. Lastly, the company elevated its 2026 capex outlook from 
TWD2.3-2.5bn to TWD2.8-3.0bn. To support this, Gigabyte has announced the issuance 
of 40-50m new shares, implying a dilution of c.7-7.5%. We are currently reviewing our 
model and will provide an update in due course.

What we recommend: 
We have a Buy (1) rating on Gigabyte with a 12M TP of TWD422, based on a target PER 
of 15x applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key risk: earnings impact from Nvidia’s 
business model change.

2Q26 miss: Auras’ 2Q26 revenue was largely in line with our estimate and Bloomberg 
consensus, while GM was QoQ down, which we attribute to the trial production of 
new liquid cooling components for next-generation ASIC and GPU AI servers. Despite 
the GM miss in 2Q26, we expect GM to improve in 2H26 driven by better product 
mix toward liquid cooling components. 2Q26 opex ratio was lower than our estimate, 
reflecting operational efficiency improvement; along with higher than expected tax 
expense, this led 2Q26 net income to come in 23% / 21% below our estimate / 
Bloomberg consensus.

rate and dollar content increase in AI servers. We slightly cut our 2026E GM to reflect 
the impact of trail production in 2Q26 and keep our 2027-28E GMs largely

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Auras 
Our 12m target price for Auras is based on a near-term P/E, consistent with our Taiwan 
Technology coverage. We base our 12-m TP of NT$1,353 on a target P/E multiple of 
19.4x forward year EPS (2027E). Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between 
P/E and EPS growth of its peers in technology hardware, based on the company’s 
2027E-28E EPS YoY growth at 23%. Key downside risks: 1) Slower-than-expected liquid 
cooling penetration; (2) Soft AI server demand; (3) Weaker-than-expected PC market; 
and (4) Potential competition from existing and new suppliers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 2

2Q26 beat: Gigabyte’s 2Q26 revenue was up 41% YoY / 37% QoQ to NT$144bn, or 12% 
/ 9% above our estimate / Bloomberg consensus, which we attribute to the AI server 
business expansion and PC demand pull-in amid memory price hike. GM of 9.6% is lower 
than our estimate and Bloomberg consensus, mainly on the rising shipments of 
rack-level AI server and rising cost of key components (e.g. memory, CPU, PCB, etc). 
2Q26 opex ratio is better than our estimate and Bloomberg consensus, reflecting the 
improving operational efficiency as shipments ramp up. Overall, 2Q26 net income was 
up 117% YoY / 25% QoQ to NT$6.6bn, or 21% higher than our / Bloomberg consensus.

Earnings revision: We factor in Gigabyte’s Jul revenue and 2Q26 result and raise our 
2026-28E net income by 8% / 14% / 13%, mainly on higher revenues. Our 2026-28E 
revenues are revised up by 16% / 22% / 30%, mainly on more AI servers and higher AI 
server racks’ ASP , considering the migration towards next-generation AI server with 
stronger computing power and more complex design. Our GM is lower given the product 
mix toward AI servers. Our OP income raise is smaller than the GP raise given the R&D 
investments to facilitate AI servers technology development. Our NI raise is smaller than 
the OP income raise given the higher interest expense, reflecting the company’s rising 
debt to support the working capital and capex. The company’s operating cash flow is at 
NT$79bn loss in 1H26 (vs. NT$5bn gain in 2025A).

Valuation: We continue to use near-term P/E to derive our 12M TP . Our 2027E target 
P/E is at 14.7x (vs. previously at 16.8x) applied to diluted EPS, still derived from 
correlation of peers’ forward year P/E to avg. EPS YoY (methodology unchanged), 
reflecting the sector de-rating on AI server ODMs amid the model transition and market

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Gigabyte 
We are Neutral rated on Gigabyte with a 12-month target price of NT$487. Our TP is 
based on a 14.7x 2027E P/E which is based on PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery, 2) faster-/slower-than-expected gaming PC growth, and 3) 
faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

零售等)、+36%QoQ、+26%YoY，機構產品占 34%(含
綜合機構產品及汽車機構產品約 1/4)、+11%QoQ、
+1%YoY，航太扣件(豐達科技)占 12%、+14%QoQ、
+40%YoY。三大產品均有雙位數季成長，尤以電子產品
為最，航太扣件次之，機構產品亦有低雙位數成長。 
累計 1H26 年營收比重，電子產品占52%、+14%YoY，
機構產品占 36%、-7%YoY， 航太扣件占12%、+35%YoY，
三大產品線以電子產品及航太扣件年增雙位數為最，餘
機構產品衰退幅度逐步收斂。 
三、2026 年資本支出約 8~12 億元，其中主要以豐達科
擴產為主，餘 3~5 億元，擴產桃園龜山製造基地 
神基 2023 年資本支出達 5-7 億元，2024 年資本支出達
14.6 億元。2025 年資本支出 18 億元，其中神基 9 億多
購買設備及越南廠房，餘 9 億元來自豐達科的馬來西亞
廠及設備等支出。2026 年資本支出預將落在 8~12 億元
左右，其中豐達科資本支出較高，神基 3~5 億元，主要
支出是桃園龜山設立一個製造基地，整修完後擴張強固
型電腦生產製造，Server 機架及電池封裝 BBU 亦有發
展。 
四、展望 2026 年，強固型電腦仍有雙位數成長，PC 相
關綜合機構件年衰退高個位數至低雙位數，汽車機構件
優於預期亦雙位數成長，推估營收年增 11%，稅後 EPS 
9.34 元 
展望 2026 年，整體營運方面，維持2026 年上、下半年
營收比重分別為 48~50%、52~50%的預期 ， 下半年營運
將優於上半年，全年營收成長 5%至 10%的目標可望順
利達成。其中，強固電腦全年營收仍維持成長 20%至
30%的預期，主要動能除既有軍用市場外，公用事業、
運輸及公共安全等新應用也持續拓展。其中，公司已切
入美國大型大眾運輸客戶，涵蓋巴士、火車及校車等應
用，4Q26 初也將發表新一代產品，後續動能看正向； 
與 PC 相關的綜合機構件受到 PC 產業在漲價循環下，
終端承受能力保守看待影響，全年則預估衰退 8%至
12%，目前表現仍落在原先預期區間，但下半年已出現
回溫跡象。不過神基積極拓展高附加價值應用，在碳纖
機構件方面，目前已有兩家 NB 客戶採用於高階商用機
種，預計 4Q26 開始出貨，AI 機櫃部分，神基桃園龜山
大智新廠已在準備機櫃製造所需的重型設備，產能預計
4Q26 初全面建置完成，後續動能可望逐步加溫； 
車用機構件方面，原本預期 2026 年成長 10-16%，不過
目前整體表現優於預期，預期全年成長幅度有望超標，
成長動能主要來自自動化效益展現，同時也積極布局車
用高附加價值機構件，近期切入 tier1 客戶相關車用
Domain Controller 表面處理產品，產品單價較既有產品

五、維持買進理由 
投顧預估神基 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 9.34 元
及 10.74 元，目前股價116.5 元，PER 12X 及 11X 評價
偏低，先前股價隨軍工類股退燒回檔，目前處在相對低
水準，由於每年配息率都高達有 8 成以上，推估明年配
息殖利率仍有高達 6.44%，故股價下方風險有限，故投
資建議維持買進，目標價維持 160 元。

研 究  報 告                
聯亞(3081.TT) 
產業：電子–通信網路 
CW Laser 矽光產品需求持續強勁，擴充產能因應 CSP 客戶需求 
 投資評價與建議 
聯亞CW Laser需求成⻑與後段切割測試代⼯產能增加 ， 預估2026/2027/2028
年稅後EPS為20.84/48.42/88.72元，考量SiPho技術逐步成熟以及聯亞為亞洲
最大獨立磷化銦磊晶廠，從2028年獲利評價，給予40倍本益比。 
 重點評論分析 
受惠矽光產品出貨增與後段產能開出，2Q26營收成⻑，3Q26營收持續走升：
2Q26營收季增35%至12.2億元，係因矽光產品出貨量增加，受惠產品組合轉
佳外，同時反映轉嫁磷化銦基板漲價予客戶，毛利率上升2.69ppts至57.5%，
稅後EPS為5.06元 ， 除權後股本追溯調整之稅後EPS為4.62元 。 隨矽光產品需求
持續增加以及下游代⼯廠後段切割、測試等產能逐步開出，預估3Q26營收季
增28.4%至15.7億元，稅後EPS為5.75元。 
持續拓展矽光客戶與產能、機台擴充挹注營收動能： 展望2026至2027年，聯
亞預計2026年矽光客產品客戶數量將成⻑至10家以上，其中3.2T以CW Laser
為主之矽光產品預計於2H26量產 。 曝光製程方面 ， 目前主要使用E-beam設備 ，
2026年將擴展另外2種技術：奈米壓印技術與DUV深紫外光微影技術。聯亞目
前奈米壓印技術5年前已經進入量產，使用奈米壓印技術製造之CW Laser已量
產中，2027年新增之機器設備亦會加入量產；DUV深紫外光微影技術主要用
於生產6吋以上之基板 ， DUV月產能可達數萬片 ， 量產時程則視交機時間而定 。  
 投資風險 
後段產能開出進度不如預期。

### Page 2

2

| 營運概況  
受惠矽光產品出貨增加與後段產能開出，2Q26營收成⻑，3Q26營收持續走升：2Q26營收季增35%至12.2億元，係因
矽光產品出貨量增加，受惠矽光產品出貨增加帶動產品組優化外，同時反映轉嫁磷化銦基板漲價予客戶，毛利率上升
2.69ppts至57.5%，營業利益為5.73億元，營業利益率為46.9%，營業費用較上⼀季增加約700萬元至1.29億元，原因為
隨獲利增加依比例提列之員⼯分紅費用增加，折舊費用約為7,500萬元，較1Q26增加約500萬元，匯兌收益約為439萬
元，稅前淨利5.86億元，稅後淨利為4.68億元，稅後EPS為5.06元，除權後股本追溯調整之稅後EPS為4.62元。隨矽光產
品需求持續增加以及下游代⼯廠產能逐步開出，預估3Q26營收季增28.4%至15.7億元，稅後EPS為5.75元。 
持續拓展矽光客戶與產能、機台擴充挹注營收動能：展望2026至2027年，聯亞預計2026年矽光客產品客戶數量將成⻑
至10家以上，其中3.2T以CW Laser為主之矽光產品預計於2H26量產。資本支出與產能方面，目前累積已公告之資本支
出金額約60億元 ， 主要用於擴充前段MOCVD製程產能以及購置曝光蝕刻製程設備 ， 預估2027年底MOCVD產能較2026
年底增加100%以上，另外2027年之產能將年增150%以上，惟矽光產品供需仍緊俏，公司將持續拓展產能，根據公司
規劃，折舊金額將⻑期佔營收8~12%以下，財務狀況無虞。曝光製程方面，目前主要使用E-beam設備，2026年將擴展
另外2種技術：奈米壓印技術以及DUV深紫外光微影技術。聯亞之奈米壓印技術5年前已經進入量產，目前使用奈米壓印
技術製造之CW Laser皆已在量產中，明年度新增加的機器設備亦會加入量產；DUV深紫外光微影技術主要為生產6吋以
上之基板做準備，由於該設備屬半導體等級且全球僅有⼀家公司供應，故必須提前下單，DUV⼀個月產能可達數萬片之
水準，可支應未來3~5年之量產所需，量產時程則視交機時間而定。綜上所述，預估2026/2027/2028年營收年增
155.6%/120%/73.4%至56.3/123.8/214.7億元，稅後EPS為20.84/48.42/88.72元。

增加持股：未來六個月內潛在漲幅 10~25%(含)。

降低持股：未來六個月內潛在跌幅 10%以上。 
未評等：無。

主要競爭對手：

蘇俊嘉 andy.su@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

優群 (3217 TT) 
高價庫存持續去化，NB/PC 出貨不明朗

永豐觀點 
受 NB/PC 出貨衰退影響，高價庫存去化較緩慢，2Q26 毛利率持續呈壓。再次下
修 NB/PC 出貨預估，僅在 Metal Bar 、CAMM 系列及 TypeC 產品可保持較佳成
長動能，股價評價位階低，可逢低長線佈局。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進投資建議：公司再次下修 NB/PC 出貨預估，且客戶更改記憶體設計情形
短期 SO-DIMM 需求恐將下滑，相關產品線如SO-DIMM、M.2 及 U-DIMM 今明
年動能皆較不明朗。研究部下修今明年獲利預估，預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 分別為
11.06 及 12.31 元。維持買進投資建議，因獲利調整目標價由 213 元調降至 172
元(14X 2027F EPS)。

### Page 2

公司簡介：優群成立於 1987 年，於 2004 年掛牌上櫃，主要經營業務為電子零組
件及連接器產品之設計、製造及銷售。產品廣泛應用於電子產業，涵蓋 NB、伺服
器、桌上型電腦及手機等領域。終端客戶皆為國際知名大廠，目前主要生產據點包
括台灣新竹、中國昆山、遂寧及越南。目前越南廠一期已於4Q25 投產，而二廠部
分預計將在 2H26 規劃。2Q26 主要各產品比重分別為：SO-DIMM 28% 、M.2 
26%、TypeC 13%、Long-DIMM 11%、Metal Bar 12%、其他 10%。 
高價庫存持續去化 ， 出貨下修營收獲利不如預期 ：2Q26 營收 9.47 億元(-10% YoY，
-0.1% QoQ)，稅後獲利 2.14 億元(+2.9% YoY)，EPS 2.38 元，SO DIMM 及 M.2
產品仍是受到 NB 出貨持續下修影響分別年減 29%及 18%，其中 SO DIMM 衰退
幅度較原先預期嚴重 ；Long-DIMM 部分 ，PC 出貨亦呈現衰退 ， 年減18%；Metal 
Bar 部分，美系品牌手機需求穩健及新 NB 機種採用，成長近 47%。2Q26 單季毛
利率 47.52%，低於原先預估的 49.15%，主要因黃金等高價庫存隨出貨下修本季
去化較慢，毛利率持續受壓抑。 
再次下修 NB/PC 產品出貨，營收動能疲緩：優群今明年各產品線主要動能如下：
(1) SO-DIMM 衰退情況較預期嚴重：目前筆電出貨仍不理想，客戶端持續受限於
記憶體、CPU 缺貨影響，被迫更改設計及調度機種，導致 SO DIMM 需求將持續
下降。研究部預期產品線年衰退幅度恐擴大至20%，明年產品線展望亦尚未明朗。
(2) Long-DIMM 桌機與 Server 用呈現兩極：優群去年有兩家美系 CSP 客戶訂
單，今年預計持續加量，在 R-DIMM 部分出貨符合預期。惟桌機出貨方面，同受
記憶體缺貨影響，U-DIMM 大幅下滑，使整體產品線成長呈壓。目前R-DIMM 出
貨佔比已逾 6-7 成，R-DIMM 出貨佔比提升可改善產品線毛利率 。 下修整體Long-
Dimm 今明年呈小幅成長。(3) M.2 亦受 NB 出貨下修，產品線僅持平至小幅成
長：優群目前 M.2 產品市佔仍逾 50%，高階 NB 逐步導入 PCIe Gen5 SSD、Wifi 
7，規格升級亦使 M.2 相關產品線保有成長動能，惟亦因筆電出貨較不明朗，預期
產品線僅持平至小幅成長看待。目前 Gen5-1A 佔比約 10%，ASP 較高。NB 廠因
高階新機種筆電增加 SSD 插槽，預估 Gen5 滲透率可持續增加。(4) Metal Bar 獨
供美系品牌應用 ，今明年持續保持高成長 ：目前微型沖壓件 Metal Bar 獨家供應美
系品牌手機，應用於 SiP 模組。及新訂單美系品牌 NB 應用在 Wifi Module ，其
ASP 為倍數，預期今年產品線成長可逾 30%。而在 NB 新應用上明年新機預計導
入 Wifi 8，Metal Bar 用量將呈 3 倍提升，明年成長性亦可期。(5) TypeC 穩定成
長，客戶改變新設計 ASP 可以提升：TypeC 多為專案基礎，應用在高階機種如
Thunderbolt 5 客戶採用優群產品 ， 預期今年呈雙位數成長 。 在明年部分 ， 因TypeC
產品客戶改變設計欲使連接器可做替換，預期 ASP 將可提升 50%，預期明年產品
線亦可保持成長 。(6) CAMM/CABB 持續出貨 ， 惟亦受NB 出貨下修影響 ：LPDDR5

營運現況與分析
